The built in `bool::from_str`\n/// only handles two cases, `\"true\"` and `\"false\"`. We want to be able\n/// to convert from a more diverse set of strings. In particular, the\n/// following set of source strings are allowed:\n///\n///  * `\"true\"`, `\"t\"`, `\"yes\"`, `\"y\"` (all case-insensitive), and\n///  non-zero integers all convert to `true`.\n///\n///  * `\"false\"`, `\"f\"`, `\"no\"`, `\"n\"` (all case-insensitive), and `\"0\"`\n///  all convert to `false`.\n///\n/// # Errors\n///\n/// Any input value besides those described above result in a parse error.\nfn parse_bool(s: &str) -> Result<bool, Error> {\n    match s {\n        \"true\" | \"t\" | \"yes\" | \"y\" => Ok(true),\n        \"false\" | \"f\" | \"no\" | \"n\" | \"0\" => Ok(false),\n        _ => {\n            if let Ok(n) = s.parse::<isize>() {\n                Ok(n != 0)\n            } else {\n                // Do the case conversion only if simple matches fail,\n                // since this operation can be expensive.\n                match s.to_lowercase().as_str() {\n                    \"true\" | \"t\" | \"yes\" | \"y\" => Ok(true),\n Errors\n    ///\n    /// Returns parse errors from the underlying time parsing functions.\n    pub fn datetime_from_str(&self, s: &str, format: &str) -> Result<DateTime<Utc>, ParseError> {\n        match self {\n            Self::Local => Local\n                .datetime_from_str(s, format)\n                .map(|dt| datetime_to_utc(&dt)),\n            Self::Named(tz) => tz\n                .datetime_from_str(s, format)\n                .map(|dt| datetime_to_utc(&dt)),\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn parse(s: &str) -> Option<Self> {\n        match s {\n            \"\" | \"local\" => Some(Self::Local),\n            _ => s.parse::<Tz>().ok().map(Self::Named),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Convert a timestamp with a non-UTC time zone into UTC\npub(super) fn datetime_to_utc<TZ: chrono::TimeZone>(ts: &DateTime<TZ>) -> DateTime<Utc> {\n    Utc.timestamp_opt(ts.timestamp(), ts.timestamp_subsec_nanos())\n        .single()\n        .expect(\"invalid timestamp\")\n}\n\nimpl From<TimeZone> for String {\n    fn from(tz: TimeZone) -> Self {\n        match tz {\n            TimeZone::Local => \"local\".to_string(),\n            TimeZone::Named(tz) => tz.name().to_string(),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl TryFrom<String> for TimeZone {\n    type Error = String;\n\n    fn try_from(value: String) -> Result<Self, Self::Error> {\n        match TimeZone::parse(&value) {\n            Some(tz) => Ok(tz),\n            None => Err(\"No such time zone\".to_string()),\n        }\n    }\n}\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

given fields.\n    ///\n    /// This method is able to determine the combined date and time\n    /// from date and time fields or a single [`timestamp`](#structfield.timestamp) field,\n    /// plus a time zone offset.\n    /// Either way those fields have to be consistent to each other.\n    pub fn to_datetime(&self) -> ParseResult<DateTime<FixedOffset>> {\n        // If there is no explicit offset, consider a timestamp value as indication of a UTC value.\n        let offset = match (self.offset, self.timestamp) {\n            (Some(off), _) => off,\n            (None, Some(_)) => 0, // UNIX timestamp may assume 0 offset\n            (None, None) => return Err(NOT_ENOUGH),\n        };\n        let datetime = self.to_naive_datetime_with_offset(offset)?;\n        let offset = FixedOffset::east_opt(offset).ok_or(OUT_OF_RANGE)?;\n\n        // this is used to prevent an overflow when calling FixedOffset::from_local_datetime\n        datetime\n            .checked_sub_signed(OldDuration::seconds(i64::from(offset.local_minus_utc())))\n            .ok_or(OUT_OF_RANGE)?;\n\n        match offset.from_local_datetime(&datetime) {\n            LocalResult::None => Err(IMPOSSIBLE),\n            LocalResult::Single(t) => Ok(t),\n            LocalResult::Ambiguous(..) => Err(NOT_ENOUGH),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Returns a parsed timezone-aware date and time out of given fields,\n    /// with an additional `TimeZone` used to interpret and validate the local date.\n    ///\n    /// This method is able to determine the combined date and time\n    /// from date and time fields or a single [`timestamp`](#structfield.timestamp) field,\n    /// plus a time zone offset.\n    /// Either way those fields have to be consistent to each other.\n    /// If parsed fields include an UTC offset, it also has to be consistent to\n    /// [`offset`](#structfield.offset).\n    pub fn to_datetime_with_timezone<Tz: TimeZone>(&self, tz: &Tz) -> ParseResult<DateTime<Tz>> {\n        // if we have `timestamp` specified, guess an offset from that.\n        let mut guessed_offset = 0;\n        if let Some(timestamp) = self.timestamp {\n            // make a naive `DateTime` from given timestamp and (if any) nanosecond.\n            // an empty `nanosecond` is always equal to zero, so missing nanosecond is fine.\n            let nanosecond = self.nanosecond.unwrap_or(0);\n            let dt = NaiveDateTime::from_timestamp_opt(timestamp, nanosecond);\n            let dt = dt.ok_or(OUT_OF_RANGE)?;\n            guessed_offset = tz.offset_from_utc_datetime(&dt).fix().local_minus_utc();\n        }\n\n        // checks if the given `DateTime` has a consistent `Offset` with given `self.offset`.\n        let check_offset = |dt: &DateTime<Tz>| {\n            if let Some(offset) = self.offset {\n                dt.offset().fix().local_minus_utc() == offset\n            } else {\n                true\n            }\n        };\n\n        // `guessed_offset` should be correct when `self.timestamp` is given.\n        // it will be 0 otherwise, but this is fine as the algorithm ignores offset for that case.\n        let datetime = self.to_naive_datetime_with_offset(guessed_offset)?;\n        match tz.from_local_datetime(&datetime) {\n            LocalResult::None => Err(IMPOSSIBLE),\n            LocalResult::Single(t) => {\n                if check_offset(&t) {\n                    Ok(t)\n                } else {\n                    Err(IMPOSSIBLE)\n                }\n            }\n            LocalResult::Ambiguous(min, max) => {\n                // try to disambiguate two possible local dates by offset.\n                match (check_offset(&min), check_offset(&max)) {\n                    (false, false) => Err(IMPOSSIBLE),\n                    (false, true) => Ok(max),\n                    (true, false) => Ok(min),\n                    (true, true) => Err(NOT_ENOUGH),\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::super::{IMPOSSIBLE, NOT_ENOUGH, OUT_OF_RANGE};\n    use super::Parsed;\n    use crate::naive::{NaiveDate,
